Assigned role: عقبة gathers the aftermath of prior misreckoning. Abductive move: I infer that failing the pass is itself a later consequence generated by earlier choices. Reading change: the pass is both what must be crossed and the outcome that follows refusal.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001033/B005","root_001033/B006","root_001033/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_553db29ced27dfd367df","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"90:11:3\",\"root_001033\",[\"B005\",\"B006\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Geçit hem aşılması gereken engel hem de önceki yanlış seçimlerin ardından gelen sonuç ve hesap alanıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001202/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7d64c0ef20212f7274a7","text":"3. **Drought-pressure drives the social reading.** Lexical evidence: ق ح م B003 “drought-year that drives people in,” with 90:14’s س غ ب B001 hunger/famine. Structural cue: the pass is immediately explicated by feeding in a day of masghabah. Assigned role: قحم contributes a famine-driven compulsion into settled lands; feeding answers that pressure. Abductive move: I infer an ecological-social mechanism: hard years force vulnerable people into the city’s field of obligation. Reading change: the pass is not abstract heroism; it is entering the crisis created by hunger and displacement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001202/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9baa36cfcdd933e8cd8d","text":"1. **Failure to thrust oneself into the hard pass.** Lexical evidence: ق ح م B001 “plunging into hardship without deliberation,” B002 “dangerous hard places,” ع ق ب B012 “steep pass and jutting rock.” Structural cue: the line follows guidance to the two najds and precedes the question “what is the pass?” Assigned role: اقتحم supplies the risky entry; عقبة supplies the steep obstruction. Abductive move: I infer that the previous guidance did not become action. Reading change: the ayah becomes a diagnosis of non-entry: he had orientation but did not throw himself into the costly path.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001033/B010","root_001033/B012"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b6acab5c2a2558059d00","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"90:11:3\",\"root_001033\",[\"B010\",\"B012\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Geçit, yalnız dik bir engel değil, başkasının bağlılığından doğan ve üstlenilip giderilmesi gereken bir yükümlülüktür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001202/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e082696f206a9cace993","text":"90:14 made ق ح م B003 decisive: the “hard pass” is activated by famine-pressure, not only mountain imagery. 90:20 later inverted اقتحم/open entry into sealed containment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001033/B012","root_001202/B001","root_001202/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e91cf6a76a95c677fbef","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"90:11:2\",\"root_001202\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"90:11:3\",\"root_001033\",[\"B012\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Eksiklik yolu bilmemek değil, açıklanacak zorlu yola bedenen ve kararlılıkla girmemektir; sonraki açıklama atılmayı düşüncesiz bir davranış olmaktan çıkarır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
